Let's think of active set and subgradient more. We can implement a "push-pull" exact algorithm:
- Given p in simplex, find S active lags with b_k near max (within eps). Use weights λ proportional to exp((b_k - max)/ε), not just top fixed; approximate max via LSE. Gradient g.
- Use adaptive multiplicative updates: p <- p * exp(-η g_central) normalized. Here g = h_i - 2M? Actually objective f=2n M/S^2; on simplex S=1, f=2n M. Subgradient h_i for M. To minimize M, move p opposite h. But if h has high variance, multiplicative update on log p may be more stable: log p <- log p − η (h_i - mean p h). This is gradient on log variables. Use line search/backtracking to ensure score decreases. This can be faster than subgradient with simplex. Could implement custom accelerated log-subgradient with line search. Function `_lse_log_value_grad_y(y, beta, n, prev今の?)`:
```
def _lse_log_value_grad_y(y, beta, n):
    x = np.empty(n)
    x[0]=0.0
    if n>1: x[1:]=y
    # stable softmax
    mx = np.max(x)
    # if mx not finite: return large
    e = np.exp(x-mx)
    se = e.sum()
    if se<=0 or not finite: return 1e10, zeros(n-1)
    p = e/se
    N2 = _next_pow2(2*n-1) maybe 2*n
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N2)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N2)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    d = n*c
    dmax = d.max()
    z = beta*(d-dmax); clip
    ez=exp(z); sum_e=ez.sum()
    L = dmax + log(sum_e)/beta
    val = 2L
    w=ez/sum_e
    # T = w dot c
    T = w.dot(c)
    # h_no2 = conv(p_rev,w) at n-1:2n-1
    N3=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    pr = p[::-1]; w_ext=np.zeros(N3); w_ext[:2*n-1]=w
    # More efficient: np.fft.rfft(w, N3) directly; w length < N3.
    prF=np.fft.rfft(pr,N3)
    wF=np.fft.rfft(w,N3)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(prF*wF,N3)[:3*n-2]
    h_no2=corr[n-1:2*n-1]
    h=2*h_no2
    mean = 8*n*T  # ∑p g_p = 4n*2T=8nT
    gx = p*( (4*n)*h - mean )
    # Due to gauge, maybe remove mean of gx to avoid drift? not necessary. But for y, if g_x[0] etc.
    # To reduce flat direction numerical, ensure sum(gx)=0 by subtract mean? This changes gradient? For log variables, sum should 0; numerical error. Subtract mean safe.
    gx -= gx.mean()
    grad_y = gx[1:]
    return val, grad_y
```

Implement `_conv_weights_gradient(p, w)`:
```
def _grad_log_from_weights(p, w, n):
    # w length 2n-1 normalized nonnegative sum1 approx active distribution
    N2 = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=fftr(p,N2); c=irfft(A*A,N2)[:2n-1]; c=max(c,0)
    T = float(w.dot(c))
    N3=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    prF=rfft(p[::-1],N3); wF=rfft(w,N3)
    corr=irfft(prF*wF,N3)[:3*n-2]
    h_no2=corr[n-1:2*n-1]  # sum p_j w_{i+j}
    h=2*h_no2
    g = 2.0*n*p*(h - 2.0*T)
    g -= g.mean()
    return g
```
For LSE we also need val. Could combine.

We can implement a posterior "exact active descent":
```
def _active_descent(y, n, iters, deadline):
   for it: select weights w = top peaks with exp((c-max)/eps) where eps small e.g. 1e-4/(n?) since d? use c.
   grad_log = _grad_log_weights(p,w,n)

```
def _resample_log(a, n):
   a=normalize(a); a=np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
   la=np.log(a)
   x_old=(arange(m)+0.5)/m? If values correspond cells. For interpolation to new n, use centers.
   x_old = (np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   x_new = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   lnew = np.interp(x_new, x_old, la)
   b=np.exp(lnew-lnew.max()); b/=b.sum()
   return b
```
This preserves positivity and smooth. For shapes with steep endpoints, log interp okay.

Let's define helper `_fit_theta(p, F)`:
```
def _fit_theta(p,F):
   p=normalize(p); p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
   logp=np.log(p); logp-=logp.mean()
   # solve least squares F theta = logp
   try: theta = np.linalg.lstsq(F, logp, rcond=None)[0]
   except: theta=zeros
   # scale limited to [-3,3]
   return np.clip(theta, -5, 5)
```
Features normalized to unit std, so solution scale meaningful. Clip to avoid absurd.

Function `_lse_value_grad_p`:
```
def _lse_value_grad_p(p,n,beta):
  c=fast_self_conv(p)
  d=n*c
  dmax=d.max()
  z=beta*(d-dmax); clip; ez; sum_e; L=dmax+log(sum_e)/beta; val=2L
  w=ez/sum_e
  T=w.dot(c)
  h_no2=correlate(p,w) # sum p_j w_{i+j}
  h=2*h_no2
  g=2.0*n*p*(h - 2.0*T)
  g -= g.mean()
  return val,g
```
Need if beta=0? Not. Use beta min 1.

`_correlate_p_w(p,w,n)` as helper. Could compute both c and corr in one function? For LSE we need c and T and corr. Self conv and cross conv. Good.

Implement `_eval_lse_p` carefully:
```
def _eval_lse_p(p,n,beta):
    p = np.asarray(p,float); S=p.sum(); if S<... return large,zeros
    if S != 1: p=p/S
    c=_fast_self_conv(p)
    d=n*c
    dmax=float(d.max())
    if not finite: return inf zeros
    z=beta*(d-dmax)
    z=np.clip(z,-500,500)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    se=ez.sum()
    if se<=0 or not finite: return large zeros
    val=2*(dmax+np.log(se)/beta)
    w=ez/se
    T=float(w.dot(c))
    h_no2=_corr_p_w(p,w,n)
    h=2*h_no2
    g=2*n*p*(h-2*T)
    g-=g.mean()
    return float(val), g
```
`_corr_p_w`:
```
def _corr_p_w(p,w,n):
   N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   prf=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
   wf=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(prf*wf,N)
   return corr[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Can we reuse self conv FFT of p for corr? Different N maybe if N2 == N? For n, N2=next_pow2(2n-1), N3=next_pow2(3n-2). Usually N3 > N2 except small. So no.

But for n=20480, N=65536 for both. We can maybe compute both with same N and reuse p forward? Self uses p forward length N; cross uses p_rev forward. Can't reuse.

`_lbfgs_log_p`:
```
def _lbfgs_log_p(p,n,beta,maxfun):
   p=_normalize(np.asarray(p,float))
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
   p/=p.sum()
   x=np.log(p)
   x0=x[0]
   y=x-x0
   if n==1: return exact_score(p),p
   y=y[1:]
   # objective initial exact
   sc0=_exact_score(p)
   opts={'maxiter':max(20,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20}
   try:
       res=minimize(_lbfgs_log_fun, y, args=(n,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
   except Exception:
       return sc0,p
   if res.x is not None:
       p2=_softmax_y(res.x,n)
       sc2=_exact_score(p2)
       if finite and sc2<sc0: return sc2,p2
   # Also maybe check intermediate? not accessible.
   return sc0,p
```

Now features. Implement `_make_features(n,K)`:
```
def _make_features(n,K):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    eps=0.5/n
    cols=[]
    # endpoint left: -log(x+eps)
    c=-np.log(x+eps); c-=c.mean(); cols.append(c)
    c=-np.log(1-x+eps); ...
    c=-np.log(np.abs(x-0.5)+eps); ... # center singular
    c=x-0.5; c-=c.mean(); cols.append(c)
    c=(x-0.5)**2 - 1/12? subtract mean
    c=(x-0.5)**3 - mean? maybe odd
    # Actually include x and quadratic; maybe no x^3.
    for f in range(1,K+1):
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x))
    F=np.column_stack(cols)
    # normalize cols to unit norm? If std, avoid division by zero.
    for j in range(F.shape[1]):
       sd=np.std(F[:,j])
       if sd>1e-12: F[:,j]/=sd
       else: F[:,j]=0
    # Ensure bounded maybe clip
    F=np.nan_to_num(F)
    meta={'left':0,'right':1,'center':2}
    return F, meta
```
Fourier cols not centered? Their mean zero for full period but endpoints maybe; subtract mean anyway. Good. Unit std.
For low n, K maybe 20. For high, K=12 to keep D~30.

def _param_global(n, deadline, parent_p, rng, n_starts, D, label):
   F,meta=_make_features(n,D)
   inits=[]
   # uniform zeros
   inits.append(np.zeros(F.shape[1]))
   # fit parent
   if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size>0:
      ppar=_resample_log(parent_p,n); inits.append(_fit_theta(ppar,F))
   # random inits
   D=F.shape[1]
   for s in range(n_starts):
      th=np.zeros(D)
      # endpoint exponents
      if rng.random()<0.8:
         th[0]=float(rng.uniform(-0.1,0.8))
         th[1]=float(rng.uniform(-0.1,0.8))
      else:
         th[0]=rng.normal(0,0.3); th[1]=rng.normal(0,0.3)
      th[2]=float(rng.uniform(-0.7,0.3))
      # Fourier coefficients
      for j in range(3,D):
          th[j]=float(rng.normal(0,0.25))
      inits.append(th)
   # Score inits quickly: convert p, exact

   # For each init with small optimize time

def _param_optimize_theta(F, theta_start, n, betas, sub_dead):
   theta=theta_start.copy(); p=_param_p(F,theta); best_score=exact
   per = max(10,(sub_dead-now)/len(betas))
   for beta in betas:

       maxfun=int(max(20,min(500, rem/0.01))) # param cheap; use 0.01s/fun
       state=[best_score, theta.copy()]

Let's inspect continuous endpoint singular optimum. Suppose f(x)=C x^{-α}(1-x)^{-α}. We can compute convolution maybe maximum at center or edges. Use scaling δ=0.5/n discrete. For α=0.25, p0 ~ n^{-0.75}, p0^2~n^{-1.5} below 1/n; edge not active. Center coefficient ~ C^2 ∫_0^1 x^{-2α}(1-x)^{-2α} dx / n? The max c ~1/n times integral normalized. For uniform center c=1/n. Integral factor relative to 1/n? For f normalized, c_center/n? Let's compute max f*f relative to 1? The continuous C = max (f*f) where f integrated 1. For f=x^{-α}(1-x)^{-α}, C = (1/B(1-α,1-α)^2) / B(1-2α,1-2α)? Something. For α=0, C=1. As α→0.5, denominator diverges? Earlier center diverges, so C increases? Wait α positive endpoint singular makes f larger near endpoints. At center z=1, integral ∫_0^1 x^{-α} (1-x)^{-α} (1-x)^{-α} x^{-α}? = ∫ x^{-2α}(1-x)^{-2α} dx = B(1-2α,1-2α) which increases as α→0.5 (diverges). But normalization B(1-α,1-α)^2 decreases? Actually B(1-α,1-α) ~? For α=0, B=1, ratio=1. For small α, B(1-2α,1-2α)≈1+2απ^2? Normalization B(1-α)^2≈1-? Ratio maybe 1+? It increases, so center max >1; not good. def _active_descent_log(y,n,deadline,label, max_iters=100):
   p=softmax_y; best=sc
   base_step=0.05  # y units
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline: break
      p=softmax_y(best_y,n)
      c=fast_self_conv(p); d=n*c; dmax=d.max()
      beta = 2000 + it*2000? Use increasing.
      z=beta*(d-dmax) ... weights
      g=grad_weights(p,w,n)
      gy=g[1:]; gn=norm
      if gn<1e-10: break
      direction=-gy/gn
      step=base_step
      improved=False
      for _ in range(8):
         yy=best_y+step*direction
         p2=softmax_y(yy,n); sc2=exact_score(p2)
         if sc2 < best - 1e-12:
             best=sc2; best_y=yy.copy(); improved=True; break
         step*=0.5
      if improved:
         no_improve=0
         base_step=min(0.2, base_step*1.05)
      else:
         no_improve+=1; base_step*=0.7
         if no_improve>15: break
   return exact best
```

def _stage_optimize(...):
  t0=now; best local = base
  total=deadline-t0
  param_dead = t0 + 0.35*total if n<=8192 else t0+0.25*total
  # param global results
  param_results = _param_global(n, min(param_dead, deadline-60), base, parent, rng, K, n_starts=8, label)
  starts=[]
  for res in param_results[:2]: starts.append(p)
  if base: starts.append(resample(base,n))
  if parent: starts.append(resample(parent,n))
  # maybe random smooth start
  starts.append(_random_smooth_start(n, rng))
  # sort by exact, deduplicate by score?
  top_starts = _select_top_starts(starts, n_full_starts, minscore?)
  # full optimize each with split
  full_dead=deadline-20
  fracs=[0.6,0.4] for 2, [0.5,0.3,0.2]
  for i,start in enumerate(top_starts):
     sub=...
     sc,p=_full_optimize(start, sub, label+str(i), do_param_fit=False)
     update local best and return
```

Stable softmax y:
```
def _softmax_y(y,n):
   x=np.empty(n,float)
   x[0]=0.0
   if n>1: x[1:]=y
   mx=np.max(x)
   if not finite: return uniform
   e=np.exp(x-mx)
   se=e.sum()
   if se<=0... return uniform
   return e/se
```
If y contains huge values, mx huge but x-mx finite. If y has inf, mx inf, x-mx nan. Check finite y: if not all finite, return uniform? In L-BFGS, if tries inf, fun returns large. 
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return 1e10, 1e10*np.ones(n-1)
```
But returning gradient huge may break. Return zero? Better return 1e10, zeros. L-BFGS may stop.

```

Param optimize theta details:
```
def _param_optimize_theta(F, theta, n, sub_dead, label, betas=None):
   theta=np.asarray(theta,float).copy()
   p=_softmax_f(F,theta); sc=exact; best_score=sc; best_p=p; best_theta=theta
   if betas is None: choose based on n:
      if n<2048: [10,50,200,1000,5000]
      elif n<10000: [20,100,500,2000,10000]
      else: [20,100,500,2000,10000] # param few
   total_betas = len(betas)
   # We can allocate a fixed time per beta or use sub_dead.
   for beta in betas:
       rem = sub_dead - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 5: break
       maxfun = int(max(30, min(3000, rem / (0.01 if n<2048 else 0.03)))) # conservative? But if multiple betas, rem includes all; too large. Use rem/max(1, remaining betas+1)
       per_beta = rem / max(1, total_betas - idx)
       maxfun = _maxfun_for_time(per_beta*0.8,n)
       state=[best_score, best_theta.copy()]
       try:
          opts={'maxiter':max(20,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-11,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':15}
          res=minimize(_param_lse_fun, theta, args=(F,n,beta,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
       except: pass
       if state and finite state[1] and state[0] < best_score:
          best_score=state[0]; best_theta=state[1]; best_p=softmax
       else if res.x finite:
          p2=softmax; sc2=exact; if sc2<best: update; theta=res.x else best theta
   return best_score,best_p,best_theta
```
But state[0] exact best during LSE run; good. However after a beta, we should set θ = best_theta? Yes, use global best across betas for next. That may skip LSE improvements that later beta could use but exact lower? Hard. Use best exact θ. Good.

Param `_param_global`:
```
def _param_global(n, deadline, base_p, parent_p, rng, K, n_starts, label):
   F,_=features
   inits=[]
   # zeros
   # base fit
   base_res = resample base n

def _full_optimize(p, deadline, label, do_param_fit=False, param_K=None):
    p=_normalize(p); n=p.size
    best_score=exact; best_p=p
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if do_param_fit and param_K and time < deadline - 60:
       F,_=_make_features(n,param_K)
       theta=_fit_theta(p,F)
       sub_dead=min(deadline-30, t0 + 0.25*(deadline-t0) if deadline>t0 else ... )
       sc,p2,_=_param_optimize_theta(F,theta,n,sub_dead,label+'paramfit')
       if sc<best: update; p=best_p
    # full LSE anneal
    if n < 2048: betas=[10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]
    elif n < 10000: betas=[20,100,500,2000,10000,50000]
    else: betas=[50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000,300000]
    # optional active polish reserve
    reserve = 0.15*(deadline - time)
    full_dead = deadline - reserve
    idx=0; m=len(betas)
    while idx<m and time<full_dead-15:
       beta=betas[idx]
       per = max(10.0, (full_dead - time)/(m-idx))
       maxfun=_maxfun_for_time(per*0.85,n)
       sc,p2=_lbfgs_log_p(best_p,n,beta,maxfun, label)
       if sc<best: update; p=best_p
       idx +=1
    # if time remains but betas exhausted, use last beta more? Could loop with increasing beta? We can add after betas, do active polish.
    if time < deadline - 20:
       # convert best_p to y
       y=_log_y(best_p)
       sc,p2,_?=_active_descent_log(y,n,deadline,label)
       update
    return best_score,best_p

```
def _maxfun_for_time(rem, n):
    if rem <=0: return 0
    if n < 1024:
       per=0.002; cap=5000
    elif n < 2048:
       per=0.004; cap=4000
    elif n < 4096:
       per=0.008; cap=3000
    elif n < 8192:
       per=0.015; cap=2500
    elif n < 16384:
       per=0.03; cap=2000
    else:
       per=0.05; cap=1500
    mf=int(rem/per)
    return int(max(20, min(cap, mf)))
```

Now, implement `_normalize` robust:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
   S=float(a.sum())
   if S<=0: return np.ones(max(1,a.size))/a.size
   return a/S
```
For parent, values maybe up to 1000; normalize okay. If parent length 0, handle.

`_resample_log`:
```
def _resample_log(a,n):
   a=_normalize(a); m=a.size
   if n<=0: return a
   if m==n: return a
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   la=np.log(a)
   x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   ln=np.interp(x_new,x_old,la)
   b=np.exp(ln-ln.max())
   b/=b.sum()
   return b
```
If m==1? x_old [0.5]; interp works.

# maybe endpoint asymmetry: s *= exp(0.3*rng.normal()* (1/(x+eps)-1/(1-x+eps)))? Could be large.

def _lbfgs_log_p(p, n, beta, maxfun, label=""):
   p=_normalize(p)
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
   y=_p_to_y(p)
   sc0=_exact_score(p)
   state=[sc0, y.copy()]
   if maxfun<20: return sc0,p
   opts={'maxiter':max(20, maxfun//2), 'maxfun':int(maxfun), 'ftime?'}
   try:
       res=minimize(_lbfgs_log_fun, y, args=(n,beta,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
   except Exception:
       res=None
   best_sc=state[0]
   best_y=state[1]
   # also evaluate final res.x?
   if res is not None and res.x is not None:
       p2=_softmax_y(res.x,n)
       sc2=_exact_score(p2)
       if finite and sc2<best_sc:
           best_sc=sc2; best_y=_p_to_y(p2)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_y)): return sc0,p
   pout=_softmax_y(best_y,n)
  cout exact maybe state best; recompute scOut
   return scOut,pout
```

Let's further think about possible mathematical opportunity: The objective with normalized p can be seen as minimizing maximum coefficient of P(z)^2. There is connection to "Bernstein components". Maybe we can derive an explicit stationary condition and solve via active set with polynomial root finding. But time.

Could use "alternating direction method of multipliers" for max convolution: Introduce variable y_k ≥ b_k, ∑p=1, minimize y_max + p? Constraints y_k ≥ p*A_k*p (quadratic). ADMM? Still nonconvex.

Could use "semidefinite programming" small n for local active sets: Suppose active lags A size r. At optimum with full support, gradient constant: ∑λ g_k_i = μ. This implies vector u = ∑λ v_k (cross-correlation) is constant on support. If support full, v(λ) constant. This is a linear system for λ and μ: For each active lag k, vector g_k = 2 shifted p. But p unknown. However if λ known, stationarity says ∑λ g_k_i = μ for all i. This implies ∑λ_k p_{k-i} constant. This is a convolution equation for p: (λ * p) constant over i? With g_k_i=2p_{k-i}, ∑λ_k p_{k-i} = (λ_rev * p)_i? If constant on [0,n-1]. Then p is deconvolution of constant by λ. Also b_k=M for active. Hard.

Maybe one could choose active set as all lags where target M; then solve p from linear equations? Not.

`_resample_log`. Need handle a.size==0. If n==0 return empty. If a all zeros, uniform.
```
def _resample_log(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
   a=_normalize(a)
   if a.size==n: return a
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   la=np.log(a)
   m=a.size
   x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/float(m)
   x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   ln=np.interp(x_new,x_old,la)
   b=np.exp(ln-ln.max())
   S=b.sum()
   if S<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   return b/S
```
If m=1, x_old array length1 [0.5], interp works.

`_p_to_y`, `_softmax_y`.

def _p_to_y(p):
    p=_normalize(p)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
    p/=p.sum()
    x=np.log(p)
    if p.size<=1: return np.empty(0)
    y=x[1:]-x[0]
    y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=200.0, neginf=-200.0)
    y=np.clip(y,-200.0,200.0)
    return y
```
`_softmax_y`:
```
def _softmax_y(y,n):
    if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
    y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
    if n==1: return np.ones(1)
    if y.size != n-1:
       if y.size<n-1: y=np.pad(y,(0,n-1-y.size))
       else: y=y[:n-1]
    x=np.empty(n,float); x[0]=0.0; x[1:]=y
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       return np.ones(n,float)/n
    mx=float(np.max(x))
    e=np.exp(x-mx)
    se=float(e.sum())
    if se<=0 or not np.isfinite(se): return np.ones(n)/n
    p=e/se
    return p
```

`_lse_weights(c,n,beta)`:
```
def _lse_weights(c,n,beta):
   c=np.asarray(c,float).ravel()
   if c.size==0: return np.empty(0),0.0
   d=n*c
   dmax=float(np.max(d))
   if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax<=0:
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[np.argmax(c)]=1? if cmax>0 else uniform? 
       return w, dmax
   z=beta*(d-dmax)
   z=np.clip(z,-400.0,400.0)
   ez=np.exp(z)
   se=float(ez.sum())
   if se<=0 or not np.isfinite(se):
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[int(np.argmax(d))]=1.0; return w,dmax
   w=ez/se
   return w,dmax
```
If dmax<=0 but c maybe all zero? Not for normalized p. But handle.

`_grad_log_weights(p,w,n,c=None)`:
```
def _grad_log_weights(p,w,n,c=None):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
   if c is None: c=_fast_self_conv(p)
   if w.size != c.size: w=np.resize(w,c.size)
   T=float(w.dot(c))
   if n==1:
      # for n=1, c length1, w sum1; h? derivative c0=a0^2, but p sum1 fixed, gradient 0
      return np.zeros(1), c
   N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   prf=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
   wf=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(prf*wf,N)
   h_no2=corr[n-1:2*n-1]
   h=2.0*h_no2
   g=2.0*float(n)*p*(h-2.0*T)
   g-=g.mean()
   g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return g,c
```
Check h_no2 length n: corr length N, slice n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive? Python slice [n-1:2*n-1] length n. Good.

def _lbfgs_log_fun(y,n,beta,state=None):
    p=_softmax_y(y,n)
    val,g,ex=_eval_lse_p(p,n,beta)
    if state is not None and np.isfinite(ex):
        if ex < state[0] - 1e-15:
            state[0]=float(ex)
            state[1]=np.asarray(y,float).ravel().copy()
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e10
    grad=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if grad.size != n-1:
        if grad.size < n-1: grad=np.pad(grad,(0,n-1-grad.size))
        else: grad=grad[:n-1]
    return float(val), grad
```

`_param_p`:
```
def _param_p(F,theta):
   F=np.asarray(F,float)
   theta=np.asarray(theta,float).ravel()
   if F.ndim != 2: return uniform
   D=F.shape[1]
   if theta.size != D:
      theta2=np.zeros(D); m=min(D,theta.size); theta2[:m]=theta[:m]; theta=theta2
   x=F.dot(theta)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
      x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   mx=float(np.max(x)) if x.size else 0.0
   e=np.exp(x-mx)
   se=float(e.sum())
   if se<=0 or not finite: return ones/n
   return e/se
```

`_param_lse_fun` as above.

`_features(n,K)`:
```
def _make_features(n,K):
   n=int(max(1,n)); K=int(max(0,K))
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   eps=0.5/float(n)
   cols=[]
   c=-np.log(x+eps); c=c-c.mean(); cols.append(c)
   c=-np.log(1.0-x+eps); c=c-c.mean(); cols.append(c)
   c=-np.log(np.abs(x-0.5)+eps); c=c-c.mean(); cols.append(c)
   for power in (1,2,3):
      c=x**power; c=c-c.mean(); cols.append(c)
   for f in range(1,K+1):
       ang=2.0*np.pi*f*x
       c=np.cos(ang); c=c-c.mean(); cols.append(c)
       c=np.sin(ang); c=c-c.mean(); cols.append(c)
   F=np.column_stack(cols) if cols else np.zeros((n,1))
   for j in range(F.shape[1]):
       sd=float(np.std(F[:,j]))
       if sd>1e-12: F[:,j]/=sd
       else: F[:,j]=0.0
   F=np.nan_to_num(F, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   # optional clip large
   F=np.clip(F,-10.0,10.0)
   meta={'D':F.shape[1]}
   return F, meta
```
For n small and K large, many Fourier columns may be aliased linearly dependent; not fatal. We can cap K = min(K, max(2, n//4))? For n=2048 K=20 <512. For n=256 if used, K=8. We'll choose K accordingly.

`_fit_theta`:
```
def _fit_theta(p,F):
   p=_normalize(p); p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
   logp=np.log(p); logp-=logp.mean()
   try:
       th, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(F, logp, rcond=None)
   except Exception:
       th=np.zeros(F.shape[1])
   th=np.nan_to_num(th, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return np.clip(th, -8.0, 8.0)
```

`_param_optimize_theta`:
Need choose betas. We can accept `betas=None`. Implementation:
```
def _param_optimize_theta(F, theta, n, sub_dead, label=""):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float).ravel()
    p=_param_p(F,theta)
    best_score=_exact_score(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_theta=theta.copy()
    if sub_dead <= time.monotonic() + 5: return best_score,best_p,best_theta
    if n < 2048: betas=[10,50,200,1000,5000]
    elif n < 10000: betas=[20,100,500,2000,10000]
    else: betas=[30,150,800,4000,20000]
    total=len(betas)
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
       rem=sub_dead-time.monotonic()
       if rem<8: break
       per=rem/max(1,total-idx)
       maxfun=_maxfun_for_time(per*0.85,n)
       if maxfun<20: continue
       state=[best_score,best_theta.copy()]
       opts=...
       try: res=minimize(_param_lse_fun, best_theta, args=(F,n,beta,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
       except: res=None
       # update from state
       if state[1] is not None:
           if np.all(np.isfinite(state[1])):
               p2=_param_p(F,state[1]); sc2=_exact_score(p2)
               if finite and sc2 < best_score:
                   best_score=sc2; best_p=p2; best_theta=state[1].copy()
       if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
           p2=_param_p(F,res.x); sc2=_exact_score(p2)
           if finite and sc2<best_score:
              best_score=sc2; best_p=p2; best_theta=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel().copy()
       # if no improvement? still continue with best_theta
    return best_score,best_p,best_theta
```
Potential issue: If state[0] exact better but state[1] theta not finite? check.
 Use `best_theta` for next.

`_param_global`:
```
def _param_global(n, deadline, base_p, parent_p, rng, K, n_starts, label=""):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if deadline - t0 < 5: return []
    F,_=_make_features(n,K)
    D=F.shape[1]
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.zeros(D))
    # base
    if base_p is not None and base_p.size>0:
       pbase=_resample_log(base_p,n); inits.append(_fit_theta(pbase,F))
    if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size>0:
       ppar=_resample_log(parent_p,n); inits.append(_fit_theta(ppar,F))
    # random
    n_starts=int(max(0,n_starts))
    for i in range(n_starts):
       th=np.zeros(D)
       if D>0:
          th[0]=float(rng.uniform(-0.2,0.6))
          th[1]=float(rng.uniform(-0.2,0.6))
       if D>2: th[2]=float(rng.uniform(-0.6,0.2))
       if D>3: th[3]=float(rng.normal(0,0.4))
       if D>4: th[4]=float(rng.normal(0,0.3))
       if D>5: th[5]=float(rng.normal(0,0.2))
       for j in range(6,D): th[j]=float(rng.normal(0,0.2))
       inits.append(th)
    # Add one high endpoint start? 
    # raw score
    scored=[]
    for th in inits:
       p=_param_p(F,th); sc=_exact_score(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append([sc,p,th])
    if not scored: return []
    scored.sort(key=_start_score_item? item as list [sc,p,th]; need helper works for list too. We'll define _item_score(item): return item[0]
    topk=min(3,len(scored))
    results=[]
    # allocate time among topk
    total_dead=deadline-10
    if len(scored)>0:
       # maybe first do quick all? We'll just optimize topk
       for idx in range(topk):
           sc,p,th=scored[idx]
           sub = t0 + ((idx+1)/float(topk))*(deadline-15)
           sub = min(sub, deadline)
           sc2,p2,th2=_param_optimize_theta(F,th,n,sub,label+'-top%d'%(idx+1))
           results.append((sc2,p2))
           if sc2<sc: sc=sc2; p=p2
    # sort results
    if results:
       results.sort(key=_result_score_key) # need helper tuple
       return results[:3]
    return []
```
Need helper `_result_score_key(item): return item[0]` and `_scored_key(item): return item[0]`. Top-level.

But if topk=3, each sub_dead cumulative based on full deadline. `_param_optimize_theta` uses sub_dead but if first gets 1/3, second 2/3, etc. Good. However inside first, it may allocate all its sub_dead. Good.

Potential issue: `_param_optimize_theta` for first top may use all its beta list within 1/3; second gets larger sub_dead and may spend more. Good.

`_full_optimize`:
```
def _full_optimize(p, deadline, label="", do_param_fit=False, param_K=0):
    p=_normalize(p); n=p.size
    best_score=_exact_score(p); best_p=p.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=max(1.0, deadline-t0)
    if do_param_fit and param_K>0 and total>60:
       F,_=_make_features(n,param_K)
       th=_fit_theta(best_p,F)
       sub_dead=t0+0.25*total
       sub_dead=min(sub_dead, deadline-30)
       sc,p2,th2=_param_optimize_theta(F,th,n,sub_dead,label+'-pf')
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score-1e-12:
          best_score=sc; best_p=p2.copy()
    if n>1 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 30:
       if n < 2048: betas=[10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]
       elif n < 10000: betas=[20,100,500,2000,10000,50000]
       else: betas=[50,250,1200,6000,30000,150000,600000]
       reserve = min(80.0, 0.12*(deadline-time.monotonic()))
       full_dead=min(deadline-10, deadline - reserve)
       m=len(betas); idx=0
       while idx<m and time.monotonic() < full_dead - 15:
           beta=betas[idx]
           rem=full_dead-time.monotonic()
           per=rem/max(1,m-idx)
           maxfun=_maxfun_for_time(per*0.85,n)
           if maxfun>=20:
               sc,p2=_lbfgs_log_p(best_p,n,beta,maxfun,label+'-b%d'%int(beta))
               if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score-1e-12:
                   best_score=sc; best_p=p2.copy()
           idx +=1
       # active polish if time
       if deadline - time.monotonic() > 20:
           y=_p_to_y(best_p)
           sc,p2=_active_descent_log(y,n,deadline,label+'-act')
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score-1e-12:
               best_score=sc; best_p=p2.copy()
    return best_score,best_p
```
Reserve for active maybe. If budget large, reserve 80. If total small, no.

Active descent function:
```
def _active_descent_log(y,n,deadline,label=""):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
    p=_softmax_y(y,n)
    best_score=_exact_score(p); best_y=y.copy(); best_p=p.copy()
    base_step=0.05
    no_improve=0
    max_iters=200
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0: break
       p=_softmax_y(best_y,n)
       c=_fast_self_conv(p)
       beta=1000.0*(1.0 + it*0.2)
       if beta>200000: beta=200000
       w,dmax,logse=_lse_weights(c,n,beta)
       g,c2=_grad_log_weights(p,w,n,c)
       if n<=1: break
       gy=g[1:]
       if gy.size!=n-1: break
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(gy))
       if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn<1e-12: break
       direction=-gy/gn
       step=base_step
       improved=False
       for _ in range(8):
           yy=best_y + step*direction
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(yy)):
               step*=0.5; continue
           p2=_softmax_y(yy,n)
           sc2=_exact_score(p2)
           if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
               best_score=sc2; best_y=yy.copy(); best_p=p2.copy(); improved=True; break
           step*=0.5
       if improved:
           no_improve=0
           base_step=min(0.15, base_step*1.05)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           base_step *=0.7
           if no_improve > 15: break
    if best_score > _exact_score(best_p): best_score=_exact_score(best_p)
    return best_score,best_p
```
This line search uses normalized direction and step in y units. base_step 0.05. If no improvement for 15, break. It may spend many FFT if no improvement (8 each * 15=120). Fine.

Maybe active descent can move direction but exact local min with multiple peaks; direction from LSE may point. Good.

Now `_maxfun_for_time`:
```
def _maxfun_for_time(rem,n):
    rem=float(max(0.0,rem))
    if rem<=0: return 0
    if n < 1024:
       per=0.003; cap=5000
    elif n < 2048:
       per=0.006; cap=3500
    elif n < 4096:
       per=0.012; cap=2500
    elif n < 8192:
       per=0.025; cap=2000
    elif n < 16384:
       per=0.05; cap=1500
    else:
       per=0.09; cap=1000
    mf=int(rem/per)
    if mf < 20: mf=20
    if mf > cap: mf=cap
    return mf
```

`_random_smooth_start`:
```
def _random_smooth_start(n,rng):
    if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    K=min(12,max(3,n//256))
    s=1.0 + float(rng.normal(0,0.4))*(2.0*x-1.0)
    for k in range(K):
       f=k+1
       amp=float(rng.normal(0,0.25))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       s += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x+ph)
    # small endpoint asymmetry
    eps=0.5/n
    asym=float(rng.normal(0,0.25))
    s *= np.exp(asym*( -np.log(x+eps)+np.log(1.0-x+eps) ))
    s=np.maximum(s,0.05)
    return _normalize(s)
```
The endpoint term can be large; exp(asym*(logn-left?)) ratio maybe n^{2asym}; if asym 0.25, ratio ~n^0.5, okay. Clip positive.

def _scored_key(item): return item[0]

def _pick_starts(scored, k, min_len=1):
    if not scored: return []
    scored=list(scored)
    scored.sort(key=_scored_key)
    out=[]
    seen=[]? no dedup necessary.
    for item in scored:
       if len(out)>=k: break
       sc,p=item[0], item[1]
       if np.isfinite(sc) and p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
          out.append(p)
    return out

`_stage_optimize`:
```
def _stage_optimize(n, deadline, base_p, parent_p, rng, K, n_full_starts=2, label="stage"):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if deadline - t0 < 10:
       p=_resample_log(base_p,n) if base_p is not None else np.ones(n)/ n
       return _exact_score(p), p
    local_p=_resample_log(base_p,n) if base_p is not None else np.ones(n)/n
    local_score=_exact_score(local_p)
    # param phase
    param_dead=t0 + 0.35*(deadline-t0)
    if deadline - param_dead < 30: param_dead=deadline-30
    if param_dead - t0 > 30:
       param_results=_param_global(n, param_dead, local_p, parent_p, rng, K, n_starts=8, label=label+'param')
    else:
       param_results=[]
    starts=[]
    for res in param_results[:2]:
       sc,p=res
       if np.isfinite(sc): starts.append((sc,p))
    starts.append((local_score, local_p))
    if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size>0:
       ppar=_resample_log(parent_p,n); starts.append((_exact_score(ppar), ppar))
    rs=_random_smooth_start(n,rng); starts.append((_exact_score(rs),rs))
    k_full=max(1,min(n_full_starts,3,len(starts)))
    top_starts=_pick_starts(starts,k_full)
    if not top_starts: top_starts=[local_p]
    # full phase with remaining time
    full_start=max(time.monotonic(), param_dead)
    rem_full=deadline - full_start - 10
    if rem_full > 30:
       if len(top_starts)==1: fracs=[1.0]
       elif len(top_starts)==2: fracs=[0.62,0.38]
       else: fracs=[0.52,0.30,0.18]
       cum=0.0
       for i,p in enumerate(top_starts):
          if i>=len(fracs): break
          cum += fracs[i]
          sub_dead = full_start + cum*rem_full
          sub_dead = min(sub_dead, deadline)
          if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 10: continue
          sc,p2=_full_optimize(p, sub_dead, label+'-start%d'%(i+1), do_param_fit=False)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<local_score:
              local_score=sc; local_p=p2.copy()
    return local_score, local_p
```

```
full_start=time.monotonic()
rem_full=deadline - full_start - 10
```
No need param_dead. But if param used less time, good. Use after param.

`_pick_starts`:
```
def _pick_starts(scored, k):
   if not scored: return []
   arr=list(scored)
   arr.sort(key=_scored_key)
   out=[]
   for item in arr:
      if len(out)>=k: break
      if len(item)<2: continue
      sc, p = item[0], item[1]
      if np.isfinite(sc):
         p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
         if p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(p.sum())>0:
            out.append(p)
   return out
```

`_lbfgs_log_p(p,n,beta,maxfun,deadline,label)` where deadline is hard stop. Inside chunk loop:
```
y=current_y; state current best
while remaining>0 and time.monotonic()<deadline-2:

def _lbfgs_log_p(p,n,beta,maxfun,deadline,label=""):
   p=...; y=...
   best_sc=exact; best_y=y.copy()
   remaining=int(maxfun)
   chunk = 200 if n>8192 else (500 if n<2048 else 300)
   current_y=y.copy()
   while remaining>0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
       call_fun = min(remaining, chunk)
       if call_fun < 20: call_fun=20 if remaining>0 else 0
       state=[best_sc, best_y]
       opts maxfun=call_fun
       try res=minimize(_lbfgs_log_fun,current_y,args=(n,beta,state),...)
       except: break
       # update best from state and res.x
       if state[1] finite and state[0] < best_sc: best...
       if res.x finite: p2=softmax; sc2=exact; if sc2<best...
       # For next call, use current_y = best_y if improved else p_to_y(softmax(res.x))? If no improvement but LSE decreased, exact not improved; using best exact may be safer. But LSE progress could lead to exact later. Use y after res if finite else current_y. If exact improved, use best_y.
       if res.x is not None and finite:
           current_y=np.asarray(res.x).ravel()
           if best_sc < state[0]? state[0] is best. If current y score maybe not best. Next LSE start from current y (LSE progress).
       else: break
       used = getattr(res,'nfev', call_fun)
       remaining -= max(1, int(used))
       if remaining<=0: break
   pout=softmax(best_y); exact
   return exact,pout
```

Now, let's code `_lbfgs_log_p` carefully:
```
def _lbfgs_log_p(p, n, beta, maxfun, deadline, label=""):
    p=_normalize(p)
    if n<=1: return _exact_score(p), p
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
    y=_p_to_y(p)
    best_sc=_exact_score(p)
    best_y=y.copy()
    current_y=y.copy()
    remaining=int(max(0,maxfun))
    chunk = 150 if n>16384 else (250 if n>8192 else (500 if n<2048 else 350))
    while remaining>0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        call=max(20, min(remaining, chunk))
        state=[float(best_sc), best_y.copy()]
        opts={'maxiter':max(10, call//2), 'maxfun':int(call), 'ftol':1e-11, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':15, 'maxcor':20}
        try:
            res=minimize(_lbfgs_log_fun, current_y, args=(n,beta,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
        except Exception:
            res=None
        if state[1] is not None:
            st_y=np.asarray(state[1],float).ravel()
            if st_y.size==n-1 and np.all(np.isfinite(st_y)):
                st_sc=float(state[0])
                if np.isfinite(st_sc) and st_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc=st_sc; best_y=st_y.copy()
        if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
            ry=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
            if ry.size==n-1 and np.all(np.isfinite(ry)):
                pr=_softmax_y(ry,n)
                sc2=_exact_score(pr)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc=sc2; best_y=ry.copy()
                current_y=ry
        else:
            break
        used=getattr(res,'nfev', call) if res is not None else call
        remaining -= max(1, int(used))
    pout=_softmax_y(best_y,n)
    sc_out=_exact_score(pout)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_out) or sc_out > best_sc:
        return best_sc,pout
    return sc_out,pout
```
If no improvement, returns initial. Good.

def _coordinate_descent(p,n,deadline):
  p=best; beta increasing; for iter:
     c=fast; w=...; h=...; delta=h
     i=max argmax delta; j=argmin delta
     if delta[i]-delta[j] small break
     for frac in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
         amt=frac*p[i]
         q=p.copy(); q[i]-=amt; q[j]+=amt; normalize? sum unchanged, but numerical; q/_ sum
         sc=exact; if improve accept
     if no improve several break
```

def _coordinate_desc(p,n,deadline,label=""):

Need `_h_from_weights` helper. Implement:
```
def _h_from_weights(p,w,n):
   if n<=1: return np.zeros(1)
   N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)*np.fft.rfft(w,N), N)
   return 2.0*corr[n-1:2*n-1]
```
`_grad_log_weights` can use this to avoid duplicate:
```

```

 This allows LSE progress. In `_lbfgs_log_p` we already continue with res.x even if exact not best. Good.
Update `_param_optimize_theta` accordingly.

current_theta=best_theta.copy()
for beta:
   state=[best_score,best_theta.copy()]
   res=minimize(... current_theta ...)
   if state improves best...
   if res.x finite:
       p2=... sc2...
       if sc2<best: best...
       current_theta = res.x
   else: current_theta = best_theta
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficients minimizing

    2 * n * max_k (a * a)[k] / sum(a)^2

using a multi-scale, log-coordinate optimization strategy.

Key components:
- FFT-based exact objective and an LSE-smoothed objective.
- Log coordinates a_i = exp(x_i) / sum(exp(x)) with the x_0 = 0 gauge.
- Corrected LSE gradients and several exact polishing passes:
  natural multiplicative descent, coordinate transfer descent,
  and a Euclidean log-coordinate active descent.
- Parametric low-dimensional models (endpoint singularities, polynomial,
  Fourier) to find diverse basins at each scale.
- Stage sizes: small, medium, then long final search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    if out.size:
        out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (S * S))


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / S


def _resample_log(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(0, n))
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == n:
        return a
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    la = np.log(a)
    m = a.size
    x_old = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    ln = np.interp(x_new, x_old, la)
    b = np.exp(ln - ln.max())
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return b / S


def _p_to_y(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    x = np.log(p)
    if p.size <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    y = x[1:] - x[0]
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=200.0, neginf=-200.0)
    return np.clip(y, -200.0, 200.0)


def _softmax_y(y, n):
    n = int(max(0, n))
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size != n - 1:
        if y.size < n - 1:
            y = np.concatenate([y, np.zeros(n - 1 - y.size, dtype=np.float64)])
        else:
            y = y[:n - 1]
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x[0] = 0.0
    x[1:] = y
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - mx)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / se


def _lse_weights(c, n, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 0.0
    beta = float(max(1.0, float(beta)))
    d = n * c
    dmax = float(d.max())
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w, 0.0, 0.0
    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -400.0, 400.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    se = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(d))
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w, dmax, 0.0
    w = ez / se
    return w, dmax, float(np.log(se))


def _h_from_weights(p, w, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    prf = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    wf = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(prf * wf, N)
    return 2.0 * corr[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _grad_log_weights(p, w, n, c=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if c is None:
        c = _fast_self_conv(p)
    if w.size != c.size:
        w = np.resize(w, c.size)
    T = float(w.dot(c))
    h = _h_from_weights(p, w, n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64), c
    g = 2.0 * float(n) * p * (h - 2.0 * T)
    g -= float(g.mean())
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return g, c


def _eval_lse_p(p, n, beta):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e10, np.empty(0), 1e10
    c = _fast_self_conv(p)
    w, dmax, logse = _lse_weights(c, n, beta)
    beta = float(max(1.0, beta))
    val = 2.0 * (dmax + logse / beta)
    g, c = _grad_log_weights(p, w, n, c)
    exact = 2.0 * dmax
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e10
    if not np.isfinite(exact):
        exact = 1e10
    return float(val), g, float(exact)


def _lbfgs_log_fun(y, n, beta, state=None):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = _softmax_y(y, n)
    val, g, ex = _eval_lse_p(p, n, beta)

    if state is not None and np.isfinite(ex):
        if ex < float(state[0]) - 1e-15:
            state[0] = float(ex)
            if y.size == n - 1:
                state[1] = y.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e10
    if n > 1:
        grad = g[1:].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    else:
        grad = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), grad


def _param_p(F, theta):
    F = np.asarray(F, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if F.ndim != 2:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    D = F.shape[1]
    if theta.size != D:
        th2 = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(D, theta.size)
        if m > 0:
            th2[:m] = theta[:m]
        theta = th2
    x = F.dot(theta)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - mx)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return e / se


def _param_lse_fun(theta, F, n, beta, state=None):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = _param_p(F, theta)
    val, g, ex = _eval_lse_p(p, n, beta)

    if state is not None and np.isfinite(ex):
        if ex < float(state[0]) - 1e-15:
            state[0] = float(ex)
            state[1] = theta.copy()

    grad = F.T.dot(g)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e10
    return float(val), grad


def _make_features(n, K):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    K = int(max(0, K))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 0.5 / float(n)
    cols = []

    c = -np.log(x + eps)
    c = c - c.mean()
    cols.append(c)

    c = -np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    c = c - c.mean()
    cols.append(c)

    c = -np.log(np.abs(x - 0.5) + eps)
    c = c - c.mean()
    cols.append(c)

    for power in (1, 2, 3):
        c = x ** power
        c = c - c.mean()
        cols.append(c)

    for f in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x
        c = np.cos(ang)
        c = c - c.mean()
        cols.append(c)
        c = np.sin(ang)
        c = c - c.mean()
        cols.append(c)

    if not cols:
        F = np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        F = np.column_stack(cols)
        F = F.astype(np.float64)

    for j in range(F.shape[1]):
        sd = float(np.std(F[:, j]))
        if sd > 1e-12:
            F[:, j] /= sd
        else:
            F[:, j] = 0.0

    F = np.nan_to_num(F, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    F = np.clip(F, -10.0, 10.0)
    return F, {"D": F.shape[1]}


def _fit_theta(p, F):
    p = _normalize(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    logp = np.log(p)
    logp -= float(logp.mean())
    try:
        th = np.linalg.lstsq(F, logp, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        th = np.zeros(F.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    th = np.nan_to_num(th, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(th, -8.0, 8.0)


def _maxfun_for_time(rem, n):
    rem = float(max(0.0, rem))
    if rem <= 0.0:
        return 0
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n < 1024:
        per = 0.003
        cap = 5000
    elif n < 2048:
        per = 0.006
        cap = 3500
    elif n < 4096:
        per = 0.012
        cap = 2500
    elif n < 8192:
        per = 0.025
        cap = 2000
    elif n < 16384:
        per = 0.05
        cap = 1500
    else:
        per = 0.09
        cap = 1000
    mf = int(rem / per)
    if mf < 20:
        mf = 20
    if mf > cap:
        mf = cap
    return mf


def _lbfgs_log_p(p, n, beta, maxfun, deadline, label=""):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return _exact_score(p), p

    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    y = _p_to_y(p)

    best_sc = _exact_score(p)
    best_y = y.copy()
    current_y = y.copy()
    remaining = int(max(0, maxfun))

    if n > 16384:
        chunk = 150
    elif n > 8192:
        chunk = 250
    elif n < 2048:
        chunk = 500
    else:
        chunk = 350

    while remaining > 0 and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 2.0:
        call = max(20, min(remaining, chunk))
        state = [float(best_sc), best_y.copy()]
        opts = {
            "maxiter": max(10, call // 2),
            "maxfun": int(call),
            "ftol": 1e-11,
            "gtol": 1e-9,
            "maxls": 15,
            "maxcor": 20,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_log_fun,
                current_y,
                args=(n, beta, state),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options=opts,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if state[1] is not None:
            st_y = np.asarray(state[1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            st_sc = float(state[0])
            if st_y.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(st_y)) and np.isfinite(st_sc):
                if st_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = st_sc
                    best_y = st_y.copy()

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            ry = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if ry.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(ry)):
                pr = _softmax_y(ry, n)
                sc2 = _exact_score(pr)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_y = ry.copy()
                current_y = ry
        else:
            break

        used = int(getattr(res, "nfev", call)) if res is not None else int(call)
        remaining -= max(1, used)

    pout = _softmax_y(best_y, n)
    sc_out = _exact_score(pout)
    if not np.isfinite(sc_out) or sc_out > best_sc:
        return best_sc, pout
    return sc_out, pout


def _param_optimize_theta(F, theta, n, sub_dead, label=""):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = _param_p(F, theta)
    best_score = _exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_theta = theta.copy()
    current_theta = theta.copy()

    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n < 2048:
        betas = [10, 50, 200, 1000, 5000]
    elif n < 10000:
        betas = [20, 100, 500, 2000, 10000]
    else:
        betas = [30, 150, 800, 4000, 20000]

    m = len(betas)
    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        rem = float(sub_dead) - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        per = rem / float(max(1, m - idx))
        maxfun = _maxfun_for_time(per * 0.85, n)
        if maxfun < 20:
            continue
        state = [float(best_score), best_theta.copy()]
        opts = {
            "maxiter": max(10, maxfun // 2),
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "ftol": 1e-11,
            "gtol": 1e-9,
            "maxls": 15,
            "maxcor": 20,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _param_lse_fun,
                current_theta,
                args=(F, n, beta, state),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options=opts,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if state[1] is not None:
            st_th = np.asarray(state[1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            st_sc = float(state[0])
            if st_th.size == F.shape[1] and np.all(np.isfinite(st_th)) and np.isfinite(st_sc):
                if st_sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    pr = _param_p(F, st_th)
                    sc2 = _exact_score(pr)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= st_sc + 1e-12:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best_p = pr
                        best_theta = st_th.copy()

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            rx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if rx.size == F.shape[1] and np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
                pr = _param_p(F, rx)
                sc2 = _exact_score(pr)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_p = pr
                    best_theta = rx.copy()
                current_theta = rx

    return float(best_score), best_p, best_theta


def _scored_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _random_smooth_start(n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    K = int(min(12, max(3, n // 256)))
    s = 1.0 + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4)) * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    for k in range(K):
        f = k + 1
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        s += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x + ph)
    eps = 0.5 / float(n)
    asym = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
    s *= np.exp(asym * (-np.log(x + eps) + np.log(1.0 - x + eps)))
    s = np.maximum(s, 0.05)
    return _normalize(s)


def _param_global(n, deadline, base_p, parent_p, rng, K, n_starts, label=""):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = float(deadline)
    if deadline - t0 < 5.0:
        return []

    F, _ = _make_features(n, K)
    D = F.shape[1]
    inits = []

    inits.append(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))

    if base_p is not None and np.size(base_p) > 0:
        pbase = _resample_log(base_p, n)
        inits.append(_fit_theta(pbase, F))

    if parent_p is not None and np.size(parent_p) > 0:
        ppar = _resample_log(parent_p, n)
        inits.append(_fit_theta(ppar, F))

    presets = [
        [0.6, 0.0, -0.3, 0.4, 0.0, 0.0],
        [0.0, 0.6, 0.0, -0.4, 0.0, 0.0],
        [0.4, 0.4, -0.4, 0.0, 0.1, 0.0],
        [0.2, 0.2, -0.6, 0.0, 0.3, 0.0],
        [-0.2, -0.2, 0.3, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
    ]
    for pr in presets:
        th = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(min(D, len(pr))):
            th[j] = float(pr[j])
        inits.append(th)

    n_starts = int(max(0, n_starts))
    for _ in range(n_starts):
        th = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        if D > 0:
            th[0] = float(rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.6))
        if D > 1:
            th[1] = float(rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.6))
        if D > 2:
            th[2] = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.2))
        if D > 3:
            th[3] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4))
        if D > 4:
            th[4] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3))
        if D > 5:
            th[5] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.2))
        for j in range(6, D):
            th[j] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.2))
        inits.append(th)

    scored = []
    for th in inits:
        p = _param_p(F, th)
        sc = _exact_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append([sc, p, th])

    if not scored:
        return []

    scored.sort(key=_scored_key)
    topk = min(3, len(scored))
    results = []
    for idx in range(topk):
        sc, p, th = scored[idx]
        frac = (idx + 1) / float(topk)
        sub = t0 + frac * max(0.0, deadline - 15.0)
        sub = min(sub, deadline)
        sc2, p2, _ = _param_optimize_theta(F, th, n, sub, label + "-top%d" % (idx + 1))
        if np.isfinite(sc2):
            results.append((sc2, p2))

    if results:
        results.sort(key=_scored_key)
        return results[:3]
    return []


def _natural_descent(p, n, deadline, label=""):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return _exact_score(p), p
    best_score = _exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    base = 0.18
    no_improve = 0

    for it in range(80):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
            break
        c = _fast_self_conv(best_p)
        beta = 1000.0 + 1000.0 * float(it)
        if beta > 200000.0:
            beta = 200000.0
        w, dmax, logse = _lse_weights(c, n, beta)
        h = _h_from_weights(best_p, w, n)
        T = float(w.dot(c))
        delta = h - 2.0 * T
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-14:
            break

        improved = False
        alpha = base
        for _ in range(10):
            z = -alpha * delta / scale
            z = np.clip(z, -2.0, 2.0)
            q = best_p * np.exp(z)
            S = float(q.sum())
            if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
                alpha *= 0.7
                continue
            q = q / S
            sc = _exact_score(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = q.copy()
                base = min(0.35, base * 1.1)
                improved = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.7

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            base *= 0.8
            if no_improve > 12:
                break

    return float(best_score), best_p


def _coordinate_desc(p, n, deadline, label=""):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return _exact_score(p), p
    best_score = _exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    no_improve = 0

    for it in range(80):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
            break
        c = _fast_self_conv(best_p)
        beta = 800.0 + 1000.0 * float(it)
        if beta > 200000.0:
            beta = 200000.0
        w, dmax, logse = _lse_weights(c, n, beta)
        h = _h_from_weights(best_p, w, n)
        hmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
            break

        mask = best_p > 1e-6
        if np.any(mask):
            idxs = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            i = int(idxs[np.argmax(h[idxs])])
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
        j = int(np.argmin(h))
        if i == j:
            i = 0
            j = n - 1

        if h[i] - h[j] <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, hmax):
            break

        improved = False
        for frac in (0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.07, 0.03, 0.015):
            amt = float(frac) * float(best_p[i])
            if amt <= 0.0:
                continue
            q = best_p.copy()
            q[i] -= amt
            q[j] += amt
            S = float(q.sum())
            if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
                continue
            q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
            S = float(q.sum())
            if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
                continue
            q = q / S
            sc = _exact_score(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = q.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 12:
                break

    return float(best_score), best_p


def _active_descent_log(y, n, deadline, label=""):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 1:
        p = _softmax_y(y, n)
        return _exact_score(p), p
    best_y = y.copy()
    best_p = _softmax_y(best_y, n)
    best_score = _exact_score(best_p)
    base_step = 0.05
    no_improve = 0

    for it in range(160):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
            break
        p = _softmax_y(best_y, n)
        c = _fast_self_conv(p)
        beta = 1000.0 + 2000.0 * float(it)
        if beta > 250000.0:
            beta = 250000.0
        w, dmax, logse = _lse_weights(c, n, beta)
        g, _ = _grad_log_weights(p, w, n, c)
        gy = g[1:]
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(gy))
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn <= 1e-12:
            break
        direction = -gy / gn
        step = base_step
        improved = False
        for _ in range(8):
            yy = best_y + step * direction
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(yy)):
                step *= 0.5
                continue
            p2 = _softmax_y(yy, n)
            sc2 = _exact_score(p2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc2
                best_y = yy.copy()
                best_p = p2.copy()
                base_step = min(0.15, base_step * 1.05)
                improved = True
                break
            step *= 0.5
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            base_step *= 0.7
            if no_improve > 15:
                break

    sc_final = _exact_score(best_p)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_score:
        best_score = sc_final
    return float(best_score), best_p


def _full_optimize(p, deadline, label="", do_param_fit=False, param_K=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    best_score = _exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(deadline) - t0)

    if do_param_fit and param_K > 0 and total > 80.0:
        F, _ = _make_features(n, int(param_K))
        th = _fit_theta(best_p, F)
        sub_dead = t0 + 0.22 * total
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, float(deadline) - 30.0)
        sc, p2, _ = _param_optimize_theta(F, th, n, sub_dead, label + "-pf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p2.copy()

    if n > 1 and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 40.0:
        if n < 2048:
            betas = [10, 50, 200, 1000, 5000, 20000]
        elif n < 10000:
            betas = [20, 100, 500, 2000, 10000, 50000]
        else:
            betas = [50, 250, 1200, 6000, 30000, 150000, 600000]

        polish_reserve = min(120.0, 0.14 * max(1.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic()))
        full_dead = min(float(deadline) - 10.0, float(deadline) - polish_reserve)
        m = len(betas)
        idx = 0
        while idx < m and time.monotonic() < full_dead - 15.0:
            beta = betas[idx]
            rem = full_dead - time.monotonic()
            per = rem / float(max(1, m - idx))
            maxfun = _maxfun_for_time(per * 0.85, n)
            beta_dead = min(full_dead, time.monotonic() + per * 1.15)
            if maxfun >= 20:
                sc, p2 = _lbfgs_log_p(best_p, n, beta, maxfun, beta_dead, label + "-b%d" % int(beta))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_p = p2.copy()
            idx += 1

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 25.0:
        ind = min(float(deadline) - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
        sc, p2 = _natural_descent(best_p, n, ind, label + "-nat")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p2.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 18.0:
        ind = min(float(deadline) - 15.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
        sc, p2 = _coordinate_desc(best_p, n, ind, label + "-coord")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p2.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 10.0:
        y = _p_to_y(best_p)
        ind = min(float(deadline) - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 25.0)
        sc, p2 = _active_descent_log(y, n, ind, label + "-act")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p2.copy()

    best_p = _normalize(best_p)
    return float(best_score), best_p


def _pick_starts(scored, k):
    if not scored:
        return []
    arr = list(scored)
    arr.sort(key=_scored_key)
    out = []
    for item in arr:
        if len(out) >= k:
            break
        if len(item) < 2:
            continue
        sc = float(item[0])
        p = np.asarray(item[1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        if p.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            continue
        S = float(p.sum())
        if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
            continue
        out.append(p / S)
    return out


def _stage_optimize(n, deadline, base_p, parent_p, rng, K, n_full_starts=2, label="", param_frac=0.35):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = float(deadline)
    if deadline - t0 < 15.0:
        if base_p is not None and np.size(base_p) > 0:
            p = _resample_log(base_p, n)
        else:
            p = np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
        return _exact_score(p), p

    if base_p is not None and np.size(base_p) > 0:
        local_p = _resample_log(base_p, n)
    else:
        local_p = np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    local_score = _exact_score(local_p)

    param_dead = t0 + float(max(0.0, min(0.6, param_frac))) * (deadline - t0)
    if deadline - param_dead < 30.0:
        param_dead = deadline - 30.0
    if param_dead - t0 > 25.0:
        param_results = _param_global(
            n,
            param_dead,
            local_p,
            parent_p,
            rng,
            int(K),
            8 if n < 10000 else 6,
            label + "-param",
        )
    else:
        param_results = []

    starts = []
    for res in param_results[:2]:
        if len(res) >= 2 and np.isfinite(res[0]):
            starts.append((float(res[0]), np.asarray(res[1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()))
    starts.append((local_score, local_p))

    if parent_p is not None and np.size(parent_p) > 0:
        ppar = _resample_log(parent_p, n)
        starts.append((_exact_score(ppar), ppar))

    rs = _random_smooth_start(n, rng)
    starts.append((_exact_score(rs), rs))

    k_full = max(1, min(3, int(n_full_starts), len(starts)))
    top_starts = _pick_starts(starts, k_full)
    if not top_starts:
        top_starts = [local_p]

    full_start = time.monotonic()
    rem_full = deadline - full_start - 10.0
    if rem_full > 40.0:
        if len(top_starts) == 1:
            fracs = [1.0]
        elif len(top_starts) == 2:
            fracs = [0.62, 0.38]
        else:
            fracs = [0.52, 0.30, 0.18]

        cum = 0.0
        for i, p in enumerate(top_starts):
            if i >= len(fracs):
                break
            cum += fracs[i]
            sub_dead = full_start + cum * rem_full
            sub_dead = min(sub_dead, deadline)
            if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 10.0:
                continue
            try:
                sc, p2 = _full_optimize(p, sub_dead, label + "-start%d" % (i + 1), do_param_fit=False)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
                local_score = sc
                local_p = p2.copy()

    local_p = _normalize(local_p)
    return float(local_score), local_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t_start + budget - 35.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = _normalize(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        global_seq = parent.copy()
        global_score = _exact_score(global_seq)
    else:
        global_seq = _random_smooth_start(4096, rng)
        global_score = _exact_score(global_seq)

    print("parent: %s" % ("yes" if parent is not None else "no"))
    print("initial internal score: %.10f" % global_score)

    total = max(1.0, deadline - t_start)
    if total < 60.0:
        out = _normalize(global_seq)
        return [float(x) for x in out]

    t1_dead = t_start + 0.16 * total
    t2_dead = t_start + 0.40 * total
    t3_dead = t_start + 0.90 * total

    # Stage 1: medium-small, useful for finding a better continuous profile.
    try:
        sc, seq = _stage_optimize(2048, t1_dead, global_seq, parent, rng, 18, 2, "s1-2048", 0.35)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
            global_score = sc
            global_seq = seq.copy()
            print("after s1-2048: %.10f" % global_score)
    except Exception as e:
        print("stage1 error: %r" % e)

    # Stage 2: medium scale.
    try:
        sc, seq = _stage_optimize(8192, t2_dead, global_seq, parent, rng, 12, 2, "s2-8192", 0.32)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
            global_score = sc
            global_seq = seq.copy()
            print("after s2-8192: %.10f" % global_score)
    except Exception as e:
        print("stage2 error: %r" % e)

    # Final large scale.  Stay at moderate length so the external evaluator remains fast.
    final_n = 20480
    if parent is None:
        final_n = 16384
    if total < 700.0:
        final_n = 8192

    try:
        sc, seq = _stage_optimize(final_n, t3_dead, global_seq, parent, rng, 12, 3, "s3-final", 0.25)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
            global_score = sc
            global_seq = seq.copy()
            print("after s3-final n=%d: %.10f" % (final_n, global_score))
    except Exception as e:
        print("stage3 error: %r" % e)

    # Secondary check at another respectable length.
    secondary_n = 16384 if final_n != 16384 else 8192
    if total > 900.0 and time.monotonic() < t3_dead + 0.08 * total:
        try:
            sec_dead = min(deadline - 60.0, t3_dead + 0.08 * total)
            sc, seq = _stage_optimize(secondary_n, sec_dead, global_seq, parent, rng, 10, 1, "sec", 0.30)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
                global_score = sc
                global_seq = seq.copy()
                print("after sec n=%d: %.10f" % (secondary_n, global_score))
        except Exception as e:
            print("secondary error: %r" % e)

    # Last squeeze on the current best sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        try:
            sc, seq = _full_optimize(global_seq, deadline, "last-polish", do_param_fit=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
                global_score = sc
                global_seq = seq.copy()
                print("after last-polish: %.10f" % global_score)
        except Exception as e:
            print("last polish error: %r" % e)

    out_arr = np.asarray(global_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S <= 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        S2 = float(out_arr.sum())
        if S2 > 0.0:
            out_arr = out_arr / S2

    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    final_internal = _exact_score(out)
    print("FINAL internal: %.10f len=%d time=%.1fs" % (final_internal, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start))
    return out
```
